Remove commas but keep currency symbol (e.g. ₩450,000,000 -> ₩450000000)\n        s = s.replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return s\n\n\nMONTH_RANGE_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"(?P<sy>\\d{4})[.\\-](?P<sm>\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(?P<ey>\\d{4})[.\\-](?P<em>\\d{2})\"\n)\nMONTH_SINGLE_RE = re.compile(r\"(?P<y>\\d{4})[.\\-](?P<m>\\d{2})\")\n\n\ndef compute_months_span(text: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Compute inclusive month span from a date range in `text`.\n\n    Supports:\n      - YYYY.MM ~ YYYY.MM\n      - YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM\n      - Single month YYYY.MM or YYYY-MM (returns 1)\n\n    Returns None if no month info is found.\n    \"\"\"\n    m = MONTH_RANGE_RE.search(text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm = int(m.group(\"sy\")), int(m.group(\"sm\"))\n        ey, em = int(m.group(\"ey\")), int(m.group(\"em\"))\n        # Replace only placeholders that exist.\n        replaced = combined\n\n        def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n            key = m.group(1)\n            token = \"{{\" + key + \"}}\"\n            return replacements.get(token, token)\n\n        replaced = re.sub(r\"\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}\", repl, replaced)\n\n        replaced, phase_appended = append_phase_months_if_needed(combined, replaced)\n\n        changed = (replaced != combined) or phase_appended or (len(t_elems) > 1)\n        if not changed:\n            continue\n\n        # Write full replaced text into the first <hp:t>, clear the rest.\n        t_elems[0].text = replaced\n        for t in t_elems[1:]:\n            t.text = \"\"\n\n        # Remove stale layout caches in modified paragraphs.\n        removed_any = False\n        for child in list(p):\n            ln = _localname(child.tag)\n            if ln in (\"lineSegArray\", \"linesegarray\", \"lineSegarray\", \"LineSegArray\"):\n                p.remove(child)\n                removed_any = True\n        #
